# Parity inventory: what the app must keep of today's page and desktop window

The parity reference of the React Native rewrite (goal 4.3, 7.1): every user-visible behaviour of today's page
(`web/src`) and of the desktop app's window, grouped by area, each mapped to the checks and tests that cover it today.
A package of `packages.md` names the rows it makes true on the new build; a ported browser check (architecture AD-80)
proves at least the rows its old check covers. Written from the code and the tests at commit `a71f4ca`, without running
them.

## How to use it

- **IDs** are stable: `<area>-<sub-area>-<nn>`. Areas: **A** the shell (sidebar, sections, Servers, layout, themes,
  keys, dialogs, storage, desktop window), **B** the chat view, **C** the composer and the prompt library, **D** the
  board, pictures, the transport and the desktop window's board side.
- **Covered today by**: a browser check by its id (`SEC.2`, in `web/dev/checks/`), a unit test as
  `test/<file>.test.ts "its name"` (relative to `web/`, which F2 moves to `packages/core/test/`), a free e2e script as
  `e2e/<name> step "…"`, a desktop test by its path. `(paid)` or `app.e2e` is `web/e2e/app.e2e.mjs`, never run unless
  the user asks. **`none`** means nothing checks it today: the new build must still keep it, and the package that
  builds it adds a check where a browser can show it (goal 7).
- **Note for the app**: `all`, `wide only` (tablet and desktop layouts), `desktop only` (the Electron window),
  `not on phone/tablet` (This computer, the owner's things: goal 4.1), `touch: …` (what replaces a mouse or keyboard
  gesture), `web view` (the board on phone and tablet), `device test` (goal 7.4). Each section's first lines say how
  it uses them.
- Each section ends with a table **"Checks and tests of this area"**: every cited check or test → the rows it
  covers. That table is what a package uses to port a check.

## Size

| Area | Rows | `none` (nothing covers it) |
|---|---|---|
| A. Shell | 208 | 10 (more partly) |
| B. Chat view | 235 | 37 (46 more partly) |
| C. Composer | 237 | 31 (8 more partly) |
| D. Board | 171 | 30 (19 more partly; 7 covered only by the paid script) |
| **Total** | **851** | **108** |

## Findings that change or sharpen the plan

1. **The page has no narrow layout today** (A): a row of panes (sidebar 200–480 px, chat panel 300–760 px, a stage of
   at least 320 px) and one resizer. The phone stack and the breakpoints of AD-72 are new; they have no row to port
   and get the new checks `L2` and the viewport runs of AD-80.
2. **Hover-only actions and drag-only moves** (A-SB-35, A-DD-01): the row "+" and "…" appear under the pointer, and a
   plain chat is moved between groups by drag only. On touch the actions show always or on a long press, and the
   long-press drag of AD-70 is the move (no new menu item: goal 4.3, no feature added).
3. **No free browser check covers an agent's board tools** (D): the mock never sends `rpc`, `release_request` or
   `superseded`, and the unit tests of `get_image` run on a fake Excalidraw. On the new build the board runs through
   the bridge (AD-51), so this gap matters more: package **B4** adds an `rpc` knob to the mock (a test tool, not the
   contract) and a check `L6-agenttools.mjs` that drives `apply`, `delete_elements`, `get_image` and `show_board`
   through the page at desktop and phone.
4. **A board whose server drops stays drawable** under a banner (D-RB): only the chat beside it goes read-only; a board
   with no kept scene shows the "not connected" view. Packages keep exactly that.
5. **Chip undo is not defined today** (C-RF-20): inserting or removing a chip is a DOM edit with no ⌘Z. The chip
   contract (AD-60) does **not** require undo of a chip; it requires what the C-RF rows say.
6. **Streaming scroll has no test today** (B-SM-01, 06–10, 14: pinning to the bottom, following the stream, staying
   put when the user scrolled up). It is the long-chat risk of goal 4.8, so package **S4** adds a check for it.
7. **No timestamps and no "jump to bottom"** exist (B-TH-19, B-SM-08): the app does not add them.
8. **The Servers dialog's add and edit flows have no browser check** (A-SV): only unit tests and step 1 of
   `e2e/remotechat`. Package **S3** adds one.
9. **The unsaved copy of a draft in browser storage** (C-DR-05, 13, 22) starts fresh in the app (goal 4.2); on the web
   build it is the same storage and is read as the page reads it (AD-12).


---

## A. The shell: sidebar, sections, servers, layout, themes, keys

Sources read: `web/src` App, Sidebar, Servers, Dialogs, Guard, Resizer, theme, main, conn, store, serversapi, version, keptdrafts, BoardOff, `sections.css`; `web/src/logic` sections, servers, serverlists, status, layout, keys, localsection, remoteview, newchatwhere, theme, flavours, dropmark, waits, version, roles, tree, boardrow, labels, sel, boardsave, agentlist; `web/index.html`; the checks A1.2–A1.5, A2.x (only where they move with the sidebar), A3.2, A3.3, A6.1–A6.4, AAC.1–3, SEC.1–10, P0.x (driver infrastructure, cited once), P7.1–P7.4; the unit tests named in the table; `web/e2e` localserver, remotechat, newchat (twopages and the paid `app.e2e` read for what they add); `desktop/main.js`, `desktop/lib.js`, `desktop/test` links, closeflush, lib, ipcface, faces, preload, `desktop/test/conn/outcomes.test.js`, `desktop/e2e/app.e2e.mjs`, `plans/app-as-client-manual-checks.md`.

How to read the "Note for the app" column: `all` = every target and viewport; `wide only` = tablet/desktop layout; `desktop only` = Electron window; `not on phone/tablet` = This computer section and the owner's things (goal 4.1, 4.3); `touch: …` = what replaces a mouse-only interaction; `keyboard: …` = where a hardware keyboard exists. A row's text is what the user sees or can do; the words in quotes are the page's exact words and the new build must say the same (goal 4.3: "the same logic produces them").

Things this inventory found that a reader of the goal may not expect:

- **The page has no narrow layout today.** The shell is a row of panes with a resizer (sidebar 200–480 px, chat panel 300–760 px, a stage of at least 320 px); there is no phone stack and no breakpoint (`sections.css`, `styles.css` have one media query, in the prompt library). The phone stack and the width breakpoints are new work (goal 4.4); their tests have no parity row to port from; the rows of A.8 (layout) are the floor they must keep.
- **Chats have no "Move to…" menu**, only boards do. Moving a plain chat between groups is by drag only today, so a touch build needs a long-press drag or a new menu item for it (A-DD-01).
- **Row actions are hover-only** (`+` and `…` appear under the pointer). On touch they must be visible or reachable by long press (A-SB-35).
- **Two windows of the app are not possible** (single instance), so the drag "from another window" rows (A-DD-10) matter for a browser with two tabs only.
- The Servers dialog on a phone is a different flow (goal 4.7); the rows A-SV-* describe the dialog the page has, and the add-server steps and messages are the app's own table (`desktop/conn/outcomes.js`).

| A-KY-01 | ⌘B on macOS (Ctrl+B elsewhere) hides the sidebar or shows it; never with Shift (⌘⇧B is the chat's tree); Ctrl+B is not taken on macOS; the key is cancelled for the browser, a held key toggles once, and neither the canvas nor a text box hears it (the composer keeps its text, caret and focus; the canvas keeps its selection). | `A1.4`, `test/layout.test.ts` ("the sidebar's key: ⌘B on macOS, with ⌘ only", "the sidebar's key: Ctrl+B elsewhere, with Ctrl only", "the sidebar's key is never taken with Shift: that is the chat's tree, and neither key is the other's") | keyboard: where a hardware keyboard exists |
| A-KY-02 | ⌘B does nothing under a dialog (the confirm dialog, the Servers dialog, the library, the insert popup, the chat's tree); it is left alone, shown or hidden. | `A1.4` | keyboard |

### A.12 What the app keeps (storage of the UI)

| ID | Behaviour (user-visible) | Covered today by | Note for the app |
|---|---|---|---|
| A-SG-01 | The page keeps, per browser: the theme ("aiwb.theme"), the selection ("aiwb.sel", a board and a chat by key), the widths ("aiwb.widths"), the sidebar hidden ("aiwb.side"), Show archived ("aiwb.archived"), the collapse ("aiwb.collapsed"), the chat a board reopens with ("aiwb.lastChat", keys only), this computer's recent folders ("aiwb.dirs") and unsaved drafts of this computer's chats; and nothing else. All of it starts fresh in the new app (goal 4.2), through the one place the page stores through. | `test/sectionstorage.test.ts` ("the page reaches the browser's storage through the store's four functions alone, and has no other storage", "every key the page writes, by the place that writes it: a setting, a key of a thing, or content of this computer's alone", "the two keys that hold content are written for this computer's things alone: an unsaved draft and the recent folders", "the board layer, the canvas, the uploads and the event layer write nothing to the browser's storage"), `SEC.9` | all |
| A-SG-02 | The selection is saved by key and restored at the start; it is judged by its server's own tree when that arrives (a thing that is gone is dropped, one of a server not yet connected waits); a snapshot of this computer judges only this computer's. | `test/sel.test.ts` ("parseSel reads the board and the chat", "parseSel: garbage is no selection, and a key it does not know is left out", "validSel keeps what exists and belongs together", "validSel drops a board that is gone, with the chat that was on it", "validSel drops a chat that is gone or belongs elsewhere"), `test/sectionstore.test.ts` ("the selection is kept in the browser by key; a snapshot of this computer leaves a section's, which its own tree judges when it comes", "a selection of a thing of this computer is judged by its own snapshot, and one of a section is not touched by it", "a selection of a chat of a run in a section's tree is dropped…", "an entry removed from the server list, with a selection of it never loaded: the selection goes"), `SEC.8` (the saved selection names which of two chats with one id is open, and a reload opens that one) | all |
| A-SG-03 | The collapse of sections and groups is saved as a sorted list of keys (entry ids and group keys); anything unreadable is nothing collapsed; it survives a reload, is per browser, and is sent to no server (no call carries `collapsed`); a local group and a section's group with one id collapse apart. | `SEC.3`, `test/sections.test.ts` ("collapse is stored as a list of keys under aiwb.collapsed; anything unreadable is nothing collapsed", "a section and a group in it collapse and expand each by its own key; nothing changes gives the same map"), `test/sectionapi.test.ts` "collapse is never sent for any group: there is no such call, and no call of a group carries the word", `test/sectionstore.test.ts` "collapse of a section and of a group in it is kept in the browser under aiwb.collapsed, by key", `e2e/remotechat` step "6 collapse and Show archived" | all |
| A-SG-04 | The collapse of a section whose entry left the list goes with it; a collapsed group of a server that is away stays collapsed (not known now); a group that a held tree no longer has goes; a group new to the page shows open. | `test/sections.test.ts` ("a collapsed section of a server that is not connected stays collapsed, with its groups; a group that is gone from a tree that is held goes", "an entry removed from the server list takes its collapse with it, the section's and its groups'", "prunedLocalGroups: …"), `SEC.3` ("a group that is new to the page does not show open"), `e2e/remotechat` step "10 remove the entry" | all |
| A-SG-05 | Nothing of a section's chat or whiteboard is in storage: no draft key of a section's chat, no value with what was typed or drawn, no recent folders of a server; only the keys that name what is selected and collapsed. A chat of this computer has its unsaved draft there while its save is on the way, which proves the check would see one. | `SEC.9`, `SEC.2` ("the browser's storage names Studio Mac after the reload"), `e2e/remotechat` step "10 …" ("the browser's storage holds nothing of B") | all |
| A-SG-06 | With the first snapshot, storage is cleared of what it kept of other servers before (drafts of chats this computer does not have, and their recent folders). | `test/sectionstore.test.ts` "with the first snapshot the browser's storage is cleared of what it kept of other servers before: drafts of chats this computer does not have, and their recent folders" | not on phone/tablet |
| A-SG-07 | The unsaved copy of this computer's chat draft survives a leave during an outage and is saved when the server is back; it goes with its chat. | `AAC.2` ("the draft of the chat that was left is not kept" fails if it goes), `test/sectionstore.test.ts` ("this computer's server away: what is left goes, but a chat with a draft the browser keeps stays, and so does its copy; the snapshot brings the tree back", "the memory copy of a section's draft goes with its chat, and a local chat's stored copy with its own") | not on phone/tablet (the copy is this computer's) |
| A-SG-08 | A failing storage (full, refused) is silent: the page works without saving. | `test/sectionstorage.test.ts` ("the page reaches the browser's storage through the store's four functions alone…") | all |
| A-SG-09 | The recent folders are this computer's alone (one key); no other server has one. | `test/serverlists.test.ts` "RECENT_KEY: this computer's recent folders have the one key in the browser; no other server has one" | not on phone/tablet |

| ID | Behaviour (user-visible) | Covered today by | Note for the app |
|---|---|---|---|

| A-DK-02 | The window is 1280 × 800 the first time, titled "AI Whiteboard", and remembers its position and size between runs. | none | desktop only |
| A-DK-03 | A second start of the app ends at once and brings the existing window forward (restored if minimised, shown, focused); there is still one window. | A-DK-04 | A link the page opens (a chat link, a link on a whiteboard element) never opens an app window: http, https and mailto go to the user's default browser; every other scheme (file:, javascript:, data:, the picture scheme) and unparsable URLs are dropped. | `desktop/test/links.test.js` ("linkAction: other origins with http, https or mailto → open", "linkAction: other schemes and unparsable URLs → drop", "window.open of an allowed URL → openExternal once, deny", "window.open of a file: URL → no openExternal, deny") | desktop only |
| A-DK-05 | The window never leaves the page: a navigation to anything but the page is prevented (and opened outside when it is an allowed scheme); the page's own URL, with any hash, may be navigated to; a reload of another file or the picture scheme is prevented and not opened. | `desktop/test/links.test.js` ("linkAction: the page itself, with or without a hash → stay", "linkAction: another file, the page with a query, or a picture address → drop", "will-navigate to another origin → prevented and opened outside", "will-navigate to a disallowed scheme → prevented, not opened", "will-navigate to the page with another hash → neither prevented nor opened", "will-navigate to a reload of another file or to the picture scheme → prevented, not opened", "setPageURL moves the page the window stays on") | desktop only |
| A-DK-06 | Closing the window, quitting (⌘Q) or reloading while a drawing waits to be saved is not lost: the close/quit/reload waits for the save, then goes ahead with no dialog; with nothing pending none of this runs. | `desktop/test/closeflush.test.js` ("close with pending saves: flush, then close again without a dialog", "Cmd+Q with pending saves: flush, then app.quit()", "reload with pending saves: flush, then reload", "a cancelled close or quit is forgotten: the next unload alone is a reload", "before-quit is registered once per app, however many windows") | desktop only |
| A-DK-07 | When the save does not finish in 10 s (the server is down) a dialog says "Board changes are not saved." / "The server did not answer. | A-DK-08 | Hiding the page (the tab or window goes away) and leaving it write the boards first (visibility hidden, page hide, a prevented unload when saves are pending). | `desktop/test/closeflush.test.js` (the app half); the page's half: none | all (app going to the background on a phone) |
| A-DK-09 | A page that cannot be loaded shows the system's error dialog "The page could not be loaded: <description>" with the URL. | none | desktop only |
| A-DK-10 | Quitting leaves every server running; the app's connections end. | `e2e/localserver` step "7 the app quits" | desktop only |
| A-DK-11 | Only the app's own page may use the app's functions (a frame inside it, another window, another page gets nothing); the page gets `window.aiwbApp` with a fixed set of functions and never the IPC event or a secret. | `desktop/test/ipcface.test.js` ("every channel is registered, and removed again by dispose", "a caller that is not the app's own page (…) gets nothing from any channel"), `desktop/test/preload.test.js` ("preload: the page gets window.aiwbApp with seven functions and nothing else"), `desktop/e2e/app.e2e.mjs` step "the page was never given the secret" | desktop only |

## B. The chat view: thread, streaming, tool cards, subagents, branches

Sources read: `web/src` ChatView, ToolCard, Subagents, Markdown, highlight, MessageImages, ImageLarge, ImageThumb, chatimage.css, Quotes (SentQuotes), quoteDom, Composer (context ring and usage popover only), fork/{Chrome, Message, TreeButton, TreePopup, LabelInput, FolderHint, actions}, TakeoverPanel (board side only: it is D's, no row here), and `logic/{toolcard, threads, subagents, branches, branchview, forktree, forkpoints, forkmessage, treepopup, treepatch, labels, usage, ctxsplit, context, perms, markdown, linediff, messageimages, chatserver, sendend, attribution, waits, remoteview, prefix, status, quotes}`; the checks `A4.2` to `A4.4`, `A5.2` to `A5.4`, `A6.3`, `A6.4`, `IM3.5`, `R3.12`, `SEC.4`, `SEC.6` to `SEC.10`, `AAC.2`, `AAC.3`, `P0.4`, `P7.1`, `P7.4`, `A1.3`, `A1.4`, `A2.2`, `A2.4`, `A2.5` (their first comments and their `fail()` or `expect()` lines); the unit tests of `web/test` that are named in the table at the end; `web/e2e/{newchat,remotechat,twopages}.e2e.mjs` and, for what it proves, the paid `web/e2e/app.e2e.mjs`.

How the "Covered today by" column is written: a check is its id (`A4.2`); a unit test is `test/<file>.test.ts "its name"` (a name cut with "…" is a prefix); a free e2e script is `e2e/<name> step "…"`; the paid script is `app.e2e step N`, which is **never run unless the person asks** (AGENTS.md): a row covered only by it is in practice not covered by a routine test. Checks run on the mock (`web/dev/mock.mjs`) at one desktop-size window unless the row says another width. `S1.1` (the Subagents chip) and `PL6.1` (the prompt library's tree, not the chat's tree) are named in the brief but belong to area C and prove nothing of the thread; `P0.1`, `P0.3`, `P0.5`, `P0.6`, `P0.7` only prove the test driver, the seed and the composer's widths. `twopages.e2e` has no step on the thread. The `Note for the app` column follows goal 4.2 and 4.4: hover actions and right-click menus become a long press, selection and ⌘L become the platform's selection action or a long press, Esc and the shortcuts are `keyboard`, and a drawer on the right is the wide layout's place for what a phone shows as a screen of its own.

### B.1 Thread and messages

| ID | Behaviour (user-visible) | Covered today by | Note for the app |
|---|---|---|---|
| B-TH-01 | A chat's thread is its items in order: each message of the user is a block of its own, each text of the agent a block of its own (assistant), and nothing else is a message. | `A4.2` (the thread read as `user:` and `agent:` blocks), `SEC.7`, `AAC.2` (the messages of an open chat), `e2e/newchat step "2 the first message is sent and the stand-in replies, started on the picked model"`, `e2e/remotechat step "7 Allow is clicked on the page: the reply arrives, and B has the message once"` | all |
| B-TH-02 | A user message is drawn as Markdown with single line breaks kept (an agent's reply drops them), and an `@board` in it is a link to that board. | `test/markdown.test.ts` "slots: chips where the tags were, mentions only when asked" (mentions); the kept line breaks: none | all |
| B-TH-03 | A message made of quotes or of images alone draws no words; a message with words draws them under its quotes and images; a message with nothing at all still draws its empty text, so that the message is there. | `test/messageimages.test.ts` "a message of images alone draws no words, one with words draws them under the images", "a message without images draws its words as before: quotes alone have none, an empty message is still there", `IM3.5` | all |
| B-TH-04 | A user message with quotes shows them above its text as cards: the quoted passage and, when there is one, the comment. | `test/messageimages.test.ts` "nameText: a message of quotes and images has words, its first quote's: the comment, else the quoted words"; the card itself: none | all |
| B-TH-05 | A note item is a one-line note in the thread with its tone (muted or error); a note with no text draws nothing. | none | all |
| B-TH-06 | The mark that ends a turn (`end` item) is never drawn, and an item the client does not know is skipped (a hole in the list too); no item breaks the thread. | `A5.3` (the malformed items: no page error), `test/toolcard.test.ts` "no item, however malformed, throws: every part is of a known shape with texts in it"; the end mark and the holes in the thread: none | all |
| B-TH-07 | A message that was sent and is not in the list yet is drawn after the items in the pending look (not an item): a branch send, and the first message of a fresh fork. | `A4.2`, `A4.4`, `test/waits.test.ts` "a branch send, until its answer: the message after the cut thread, the line that the agent is starting, and no status line of the source", "the first message of a fork: after the copied history, until the thread has it, with no line of its own", `test/waitflows.test.ts` "the first message of a fresh fork: after the copied history from Send until the thread has it" | all |
| B-TH-08 | A message on its way shows its images with its text and quotes. | `test/waits.test.ts` "a message on its way shows its images with its text and quotes, when it has any" | all |
| B-TH-09 | Lines with three dots at the foot of the thread say what goes on: "Starting the agent…", "Loading…", "Opening the branch…", "Making a fork of this chat…" (and on another server "Sending to <name>…"); each has its own rule for when it shows and goes. | `A4.2`, `A4.3`, `A4.4`, `test/waits.test.ts` "a list being fetched is a line where the thread would be blank, and a branch being opened a line at the thread's foot", "a fork being made is a line at the foot of the source's thread, beside whatever else goes on", `test/chatserver.test.ts` "sendingLine: the line of a fork's or a branch's first message on its way to another server" | all |

| B-TH-11 | An idle chat that waits on subagents says so at the foot ("Waiting on N subagents", and "N subagent results not sent yet. ### B.2 Streaming and scroll

| ID | Behaviour (user-visible) | Covered today by | Note for the app |
|---|---|---|---|
| B-SM-01 | The agent's text grows in its block as it streams; a block that is still being written is marked streaming, and shows a blinking caret while it has no text yet. | none | all |
| B-SM-02 | Only the last Markdown block of a streaming text is parsed again; the blocks before it keep their text and are not drawn again. | `test/markdown.test.ts` "blocks split at blank lines", "the blocks are the text's own lines"; the drawing: none | all (goal 4.8: smooth, long chats) |
| B-SM-03 | A fence not closed yet runs to the end of the text and is drawn as code while it streams. | `test/markdown.test.ts` "an unclosed fence (still streaming) runs to the end" | all |
| B-SM-04 | A reference tag cut off at the end of a streaming text is not shown until it is whole. | `test/markdown.test.ts` "a reference tag still being streamed is held back" | all |
| B-SM-05 | A tool call's input that is still arriving shows what has arrived, and an open card follows the item as it grows (content, then input, then result) and stays open. | `A5.3`, `test/toolcard.test.ts` "an input that is still arriving is read as far as it came", "a streamed input is closed where it stops" | all |
| B-SM-06 | A thread opens at the bottom, also after another chat or another branch is shown: the scroll is pinned to the end. | none | all |
| B-SM-07 | While the user has not scrolled up, the thread stays pinned to its end as items arrive and grow. | none | all (goal 4.8) |
| B-SM-08 | When the user scrolls up (more than 40 px from the end), the thread stops following and stays where the user put it while the answer streams; scrolling back to within 40 px of the end follows again. There is no "jump to bottom" button today. | none | all (goal 4.8 `[proposed]`) |
| B-SM-09 | When the composer grows, or the panel is resized, so that the thread gets shorter, a pinned thread stays at its end. | none | all |
| B-SM-10 | A subagent's drawer follows its end only while that subagent runs; one that has ended opens at its top; opening another subagent resets the scroll. | none | all |
| B-SM-11 | Item updates arrive with a version: a newer one replaces or appends items at their index (also past a gap), an older or equal one changes nothing, and a thread that is not loaded stays so until fetched. | `test/threads.test.ts` "applyUpdates: a newer version replaces and appends items at their index", "applyUpdates: a version that is not newer changes nothing", "applyUpdates: a thread that is not loaded stays so", "applyUpdates: a gap in versions is applied as it comes", "afterAnswer: the answer is the thread, unless the thread kept is at its version or past it" | all |
| B-SM-12 | A reply that is still streaming offers no action (no quote, no branch, no label) until it is done. | `test/forkpoints.test.ts` "messageActions: a streaming reply offers nothing" | all |
| B-SM-13 | After the connection returns the open thread is level with the server: what was made meanwhile appears once, none twice. | `SEC.7`, `AAC.2`, `e2e/remotechat step "8 the open chat is brought level with B and the warning goes"` | all; `device test` for the app returning from the background (goal 4.8) |
| B-SM-14 | A stream held for hours does not grow the page's memory without bound; a long chat stays smooth. | none | all (goal 4.8 `[not verified]`) |

### B.5 Permission cards

| ID | Behaviour (user-visible) | Covered today by | Note for the app |
|---|---|---|---|

### B.14 Quoting a passage of an answer: the thread's side

| ID | Behaviour (user-visible) | Covered today by | Note for the app |
|---|---|---|---|
| B-QS-01 | Text selected inside one message (yours, or an agent's reply that is done) and ⌘L / Ctrl+L quotes it: a comment field opens at the passage, Enter adds the quote to the message being written. | `P7.4` (a reply's text selected: the box opens inside the window with the focus in its comment field, Enter adds it; the quote and its comment are in the saved draft) | touch: the platform's selection action, or a long press (goal 4.2) |
| B-QS-02 | A selection that takes text from two messages is not quoted: "Select text within one message". | `P7.4` | touch: the same sentence |
| B-QS-03 | With nothing selected in a plain chat ⌘L says "Select text in a message to quote it"; in a board chat it puts the board's selection into the message instead. | `P7.4` | keyboard: where a hardware keyboard exists |
| B-QS-04 | A reply that is still being written cannot be quoted: "Wait for the reply to finish, then quote it". | none | all |
| B-QS-05 | Only a message's own text is selectable and quotable: the header of a code block, a tool card, a message's actions and label, a quote card and the subagent drawer's items are not. | none | all |
| B-QS-06 | A quote is kept as the quoted words with the message's index and the start and end in the message's displayed text, and is found again by that place, else by searching the message, else the whole message stands for it; comparing ignores whitespace and block breaks. | `test/quotes.test.ts` "finds a quote at its stored position", "a quote across blocks matches with its line breaks", "searches the message when the position no longer matches", "stands for the whole message when the quote is gone" | all |
| B-QS-07 | The passages quoted in the message being written stay marked in the thread; a tap on a marked passage opens its comment field again. | none | all |
| B-QS-08 | A quote card of a sent message ("Show where this is from") scrolls the thread, smoothly, to the quoted passage and flashes it (about 1.6 s, then fading over 1.2 s; the colour differs in light and dark); it falls back to the first place the words appear, else the message. | `test/quotes.test.ts` "searches the message when the position no longer matches", "stands for the whole message when the quote is gone" (finding only); the scroll and the flash: none | all |
| B-QS-09 | A message that is only quotes is named in the tree, the banner and the branch name by its first quote after "❝" (the comment, else the quoted words). | `test/forkpoints.test.ts` "toTreeItems names a message that is only quotes by its first quote, as the tree route does", `test/branchview.test.ts` "bannerOf: a message that is only quotes is named by its first quote", `test/messageimages.test.ts` "nameText: a message of quotes and images has words, its first quote's: the comment, else the quoted words" | all |

## C. The composer: text, board references, quotes, images, drafts, choices, prompt library

Sources read: `web/src` Composer.tsx, RefInput.tsx, ComposerImages.tsx, ImageThumb.tsx, composerimages.css, Quotes.tsx, quoteDom.ts, ChatChoices.tsx, SubSettings.tsx, Library.tsx, libraryapi.ts, uploads.ts, chatdraft.ts, keptdrafts.ts, Canvas.tsx (pick layer), board.ts (refs, `buildContext`), styles.css (composer rules); `web/src/logic` drafts, draftsback, mentions, refs, insert, composerfit, quotes, promptsearch, library, libraryview, models, agentlist, folderagents, sendend, chatimage, subsettings, newchatwhere, chatserver, status, waits, roles (prefix, sel and image are not the composer's: `image.ts` is the board's `get_image`, `prefix`/`sel` are B's and A's). Checks (`web/dev/checks`): IM3.2, R3.12, S1.1, P0.6, P7.4, SEC.10, SEC.7, SEC.9, SEC.6, SEC.8, AAC.2, AAC.3, PL4.1, PL5.1, PL5.2, PL6.1, A2.2 to A2.6, A4.2 to A4.4, A1.3, A1.4, A6.3, P0.1, P0.4, P0.5, P7.1, P7.3. A3.3 (a drag over the sidebar's zones), A6.1/A6.2/A6.4 (the theme picker, its reload, its contrast floors) and IM3.5 (a sent message's thumbnails) were read and cover nothing of this area; A6.3 is cited only for the two library popups. `web/dev/reallibrary.mjs`. Unit tests in `web/test` (names grepped) and `desktop/test/pickimages.test.js`. e2e (`web/e2e`): newchat, remotechat, twopages (composer steps only).

How to read the "Note for the app" column: `all` = every target; `wide only` / `desktop only` as the goal's 4.1 and 4.2 say; `touch: …` = what replaces a mouse or keyboard gesture on a phone or tablet, as far as the goal names it (goal 4.2: the composer and its input with chips are rewritten; quotes by the platform's own selection action or a long press; popups become sheets; images through the device's picker and a native fetch); `keyboard: …` = needs a hardware keyboard; `device test` = goal 7.4 puts it on a device or simulator (the composer: keyboard, selection, board-reference chips; the image picker). Rows whose logic is a DOM-free module of `web/src/logic` carry over unchanged (goal 4.2) and keep their unit test; the rows with `none` are behaviours of the DOM code that no test covers today.

Unsent text and the unsaved copy of a draft that the page keeps in the browser's storage start fresh in the app (goal 4.2, "Does not carry over"); the rows below that describe the unsaved copy say what the behaviour is, not that the old copy is read.

### C.1 Text, Enter, Send, Stop, growth and the fit of the toolbar
| ID | Behaviour (user-visible) | Covered today by | Note for the app |
|---|---|---|---|
| C-TX-01 | The box shows a placeholder while it holds no non-blank text: "Ask <Agent>…" in a plain chat, "Ask or tell… (@ to mention a board)" in a board chat; it goes as soon as text is typed. | none (the read-only and no-agent placeholders are C-RO-01 and C-CH-05) | all |

| C-TX-05 | Send (↑) is off with a blank box, no quote and no image; on with text, with a quote alone, or with images alone. | `IM3.2` (off with nothing, on with an image alone), `test/chatimage.test.ts` "the composer's Send: enabled with images alone, held by imagesHold, …", `test/drafts.test.ts` "a Send of images alone is a Send of a draft: …" | all |

| C-TX-07 | At Send the box, the mentions, the quotes and the images are emptied at once; the server clears the draft of the branch. | `IM3.2` ("the strip still has the image of the message that was sent"), `PL5.2` (draft and message sent), `test/drafts.test.ts` "the composer shows the text it holds when its box appears, takes a save's answer …" | all |
| C-TX-08 | A Send that fails puts its text back into the box when the box is still empty (nothing typed since is lost or replaced), its quotes back before the ones added meanwhile, its images back before the ones added meanwhile. | `test/sendend.test.ts` "putsBack: a failed Send's message goes back unless its draft was removed on the server meanwhile", `A4.4` (text back in the box with the error under it), `test/remotechat.test.ts` "a Send that fails, or whose arrival is not known, keeps the images with the text: …", `test/quotes.test.ts` "a failed send's quotes come back before the ones added meanwhile" | all |
| C-TX-09 | A failed Send is not put back when the sent draft was removed on the server meanwhile (another window sent it, or the Send was taken after all). | `test/remotechat.test.ts` "a Send with no answer whose draft that server removed meanwhile: …", "a first message with no answer whose draft that server removed meanwhile: …", `test/drafts.test.ts` "windows: a Send refused here, the text put back, then sent from the other window, does not come back" | all |
| C-TX-10 | While the agent is busy (or a fork's agent starts) Send is off and its title says "The agent is working" / "The agent is starting"; Enter sends nothing; typing still works. | `test/status.test.ts` "composerControls: Stop shows while subagents run; Send and Esc follow the agent", `test/chatserver.test.ts` "a started chat's Send is off too while its server is not connected: …", `A4.3` ("Typing still works") | all |

| C-TX-12 | While a message that starts a new branch, or a fork's first message, is on its way, Send takes no press and shows a spinner in place of the arrow, titled "Your message is being sent". | `test/waits.test.ts` "Send takes no press and shows a spinner while a message is on its way, and names the model a fork's agent starts on", `A4.2`, `A4.3`, `P7.1` (the spinner is readable) | all |

### C.11 Read-only composer, archived and legacy chats, the error line

## D. The board, and what the user sees of the transport and the desktop window

Sources read: `react-native-rewrite-goal.md` (4.5, 4.6, 7.3, 7.4, D7, D12), `docs/architecture.md` (canvas theme, `get_image`, pictures); `web/src` `Canvas.tsx`, `CanvasFilters.tsx`, `flavours-canvas.css`, `board.ts`, `apply.ts`, `BoardOff.tsx`, `TakeoverPanel.tsx`, `main.tsx`, `api.ts`, `conn.ts`, `uploads.ts`, `ComposerImages.tsx`, `ImageLarge.tsx`, `transport/*`, `logic/{boardsave,roles,canvastheme,attribution,refs,image,context,status,sel}.ts`, the ⌘L / ⌘⇧L code of `Composer.tsx`; `desktop/{main,preload,ipcface,lib}.js`, `desktop/conn/picture.js`; the browser checks of `web/dev/checks` (first comment and `fail()` lines of `P7.5`, `A6.3`, `A1.4`, `P7.4`, `P0.3`, `P0.7`, `P0.4`, `SEC.6`–`SEC.10`, `AAC.1`–`AAC.3`, `R3.12`, `IM3.2`, `IM3.5`); of these `P0.4`, `SEC.8`, `SEC.10`, `AAC.1` and `AAC.3` were read and cover no behaviour of this area (the mock's routes, chats and sections, the read-only chat), so they are not cited; the unit tests `web/test/{saves,boardsave,boardrow,roles,canvastheme,attribution,sel,refs,context,image,status,api,transport,conn,chatimage,messageimages,keptpage}.test.ts`; `web/dev/{host,hostface}.test.mjs`; `web/e2e/{twopages,remotechat,newchat,localserver}.e2e.mjs` and the paid `web/e2e/app.e2e.mjs` (read only, never run); `desktop/e2e/app.e2e.mjs`; `desktop/test/{closeflush,pickimages,links,preload,ipcface,faces,lib}.test.js` and `desktop/test/conn/{pictures,calls,connection}.test.js`.

How to read the "Covered today by" column. Paths of unit tests are relative to `web/` (`test/saves.test.ts`) unless they start with `desktop/` or `web/`. A test name is quoted exactly, or from its start up to `…` when it is long. `(paid)` marks `web/e2e/app.e2e.mjs`, which spends money and is never run by an agent: it is reference only, it is not a free test. `none` is a gap the new build must still test.

What the browser checks cannot see today: the mock (`web/dev/mock.mjs`) never sends `rpc`, `release_request` or `superseded` and answers every take with `held`. So **no free browser check covers an agent's board tools** (`apply`, `get_image`, `delete_elements`, `show_board`, the working pill, the outline of an agent's change), and the take-over panel is covered by `web/e2e/twopages.e2e.mjs` and by unit tests on a fake canvas only. The unit tests of `get_image` and of the tools run on a fake Excalidraw (`exportToBlob` and the live API are fakes), so the real drawing engine is not exercised by any free test either.

Note-for-the-app words: `all` (every target, in the layout that fits), `wide only`, `desktop only` (the Electron window of the desktop app), `web view` (on phone and tablet the board runs in a web view through the bridge, goal 4.5), `touch: <equivalent>`, `device test` (goal 7.4 puts it on a device or a simulator), `phone: async picture address` (goal D12).

### D.1 Opening a board, and the scene in step with the server (BD)

| ID | Behaviour (user-visible) | Covered today by | Note for the app |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-BD-01 | Choosing a board opens it in Excalidraw showing the stored drawing: the shapes, the images (files) and the background colour as last saved. | `P0.3` (state "board" of A6: the editable canvas and its toolbar on screen), `P0.7` (screenshot state "board"), `AAC.2` and `SEC.7` (the canvas is drawn on), `test/saves.test.ts` "opening a free board reads its scene once: a read on its way that lands at the granted revision is kept" | web view, device test (the web view loads) |
| D-BD-02 | Leaving a board and coming back shows it as it was left (drawing, scroll, zoom) while the window still keeps the scene; the scene is kept in memory only and read again after a reload. | `test/saves.test.ts` "a lost board's saved scene is kept for a grant at its revision, and read again at another", `SEC.9` (a reload gives the drawing up) | web view |
| D-BD-03 | While the scene is being read the stage is empty (no other board's drawing shows); a read that fails says "Couldn't open this board: <reason>". | none | all |
| D-BD-04 | A board never written opens as an empty white canvas; its first save is based on revision 0. | `test/api.test.ts` "a board's scene read gives its revision from the answer, 0 for none; a save keeps the call's own keepalive and signal", `web/e2e/app.e2e.mjs` step 4 (paid) "New whiteboard in Research: files at once…" | all |
| D-BD-05 | A scene kept at an older revision than the stored one is never shown after a grant: it is read again (a read in flight at the grant, or of before the last write of the holder, is not kept). | `test/saves.test.ts` "a scene read that is on its way when the board is granted is not kept: the scene is read again, and the first save names the granted revision", "a scene read that is on its way when the kept scene is dropped is not kept either" | all |
| D-BD-06 | A shape drawn by hand starts clean: roughness 0 ("architect"), sharp corners, sharp arrows, the normal font (Nunito) for text. | `web/e2e/app.e2e.mjs` step 6 (paid) "Drawing by hand: sharp, roughness 0, normal font…" | web view, touch: drawn with finger or pencil |
| D-BD-07 | A style the user chose (stroke style, colour, …) is kept for the next board opened in the same session, not after a reload. | `web/e2e/app.e2e.mjs` step 6 (paid) "…a stroke style change carries to another board" | web view |
| D-BD-08 | The canvas offers no Open, no Save, no Save to disk (⌘O, ⌘S, ⌘⇧S); exporting an image stays. | none | wide only for the shortcuts; the menu entries: web view |
| D-BD-09 | Dropping a `.excalidraw` or `.json` file on the canvas does nothing (the board's file is the app's); dropping an image or a library still works. | none | desktop only (a file drop), web view |
| D-BD-10 | An archived board opens read-only: no drawing tools, "Archived — read-only" in the board's bar, and nothing it holds is saved or taken. | `web/e2e/app.e2e.mjs` step 13 (paid) "Archive the board; show archived; read-only; unarchiving one chat brings the board back", `test/saves.test.ts` "an archived or unknown board is not asked for", "an archived or removed board with a failed save is not pending" | all |
| D-BD-11 | A board that is unarchived while it is on screen is asked for (if free) and becomes drawable again. | `web/e2e/app.e2e.mjs` step 13 (paid) (the board shows unarchived; the drawing tools are not re-checked) | all |
| D-BD-12 | Panning or zooming alone writes nothing to the server; only a change of the drawing (elements, or the number of image files) is saved. | none | web view |
| D-BD-13 | Undo and redo (⌘Z, ⌘⇧Z) undo and redo the user's own drawing. | none (Excalidraw's own) | wide only for the keys, touch: undo and redo buttons of the canvas |
| D-BD-14 | Drawing, panning and zooming work with a finger and with a pencil. | none (by hand, goal 7.5) | touch: finger and pencil draw, a two-finger gesture pans and zooms |

### D.2 Saves, and saves before the board goes away (SV)

| ID | Behaviour (user-visible) | Covered today by | Note for the app |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-SV-01 | A drawing the user makes is saved on the server by itself, about half a second after the last change; a burst of changes is one write. | `AAC.2` ("a drawing on this computer's board was not saved on the revision …"), `SEC.7`, `SEC.9` ("drawing on the section's board started no save of it"), `P7.4` ("the drawing was not stored after a shape was moved"), `web/e2e/app.e2e.mjs` step 6 (paid) "…the hand-drawn rectangle is saved" | all, web view |
| D-SV-02 | A save names the revision it is based on (the one the scene was read at, then the one the last accepted save answered); at most one write is in flight per board and they go in order. | `test/saves.test.ts` "a save names the revision the scene was read at, then the one the server answered", `AAC.2` and `SEC.7` ("…saved on the revision …") | all |
| D-SV-03 | A save the server refuses as stale (the stored scene is not the one the edit was made on) drops the edit and shows the newer stored drawing, with the note "A change made here was not saved in time and was dropped."; nothing newer is overwritten. | `test/saves.test.ts` "a stale save drops the edit and reads the scene again: nothing newer is overwritten", "under an outage `stale` and `not_holder` drop the edit and clear the outage", "a drop with no outage keeps the plain notice, also after one with an outage" | all |
| D-SV-04 | A save refused because another window holds the board drops the edit and shows the take-over panel (the role becomes "other"). | `test/saves.test.ts` "a save refused with not_holder: the board is another window's, and the edit is dropped", `web/e2e/twopages.e2e.mjs` (d) "the held write, released, is refused: another window holds the board" | all |
| D-SV-05 | A save that fails for another reason (no answer, an error) keeps the edit, with no retry timer: the next change or the next flush writes it again; a later success clears the failure. | `test/saves.test.ts` "a failed save stays pending after flushAll, so aiwbFlush gives false", "a flush once saveScene works again writes the board and clears it", "a later successful write clears a failure; a later failed one sets it again" | all |
| D-SV-06 | A flush retries every board whose save failed; a board that was archived, removed or forgotten with a failed save is not pending. | `test/saves.test.ts` "flushAll retries every board whose save failed", "forgetBoard on a board with a failed save clears it", "an archived or removed board with a failed save is not pending" | all |
| D-SV-07 | A drawing the server will not take as it is (over 128 MB, an unreadable answer, any refusal with a sentence) keeps the edit in the window and says "Not saved: <the server's sentence>" on the canvas. | `test/boardsave.test.ts` "a save of a board that is refused for another reason than an outage (413 too_large, 502 bad_answer): the edit is kept, and the canvas says the server's sentence", "the banner and the notice" | all |
| D-SV-08 | The drawing is written when the tab or the window is hidden or its page goes away (`visibilitychange` to hidden, `pagehide`). | none | web view, desktop only for the window; touch: see D-SV-11 |
| D-SV-09 | While a write is pending the page asks not to be unloaded (`beforeunload` is prevented), so that the app can wait for it; with none pending nothing is asked. | `desktop/test/closeflush.test.js` "close with pending saves: flush, then close again without a dialog" (the page side is simulated) | desktop only |
| D-SV-10 | `aiwbFlush` (the call the app makes before it closes or reloads) resolves true only when every board is written, false when a save failed or a board is still unsaved; a board lost to another window does not keep it false. | `test/saves.test.ts` "a failed save stays pending after flushAll, so aiwbFlush gives false", "superseded: that board is lost with its unsaved edit; the stream's other boards are as before, and aiwbFlush is true" | desktop only |
| D-SV-11 | On a phone or a tablet the drawing is saved when the app goes to the background or the screen goes away. | none | web view, touch: app to background (goal 4.5), device test |
| D-SV-12 | Leaving the board on screen (another board or a chat is chosen) writes its pending edit. | none | all |
| D-SV-13 | Before a board is handed to another window (`release_request`) its pending save is written first, then it is let go; the other boards wait as before. | `test/saves.test.ts` "release_request: the one board's pending save is written, then it is let go; the others wait as before", `web/e2e/twopages.e2e.mjs` (b) "P1's edit was written and accepted before P1 let the board go" | all |
| D-SV-14 | When a server says it is stopping, the page writes that server's boards and tells it they are written. | `test/conn.test.ts` "`server_stopping` writes the boards of that server and says so to it", `test/api.test.ts` "the answer of a board tool call and the flush go to the server whose event they answer, this computer's by default" | all |
| D-SV-15 | A write in flight when the board is lost, and a write that was answered `not_holder` for an earlier role, change nothing when they answer. | `test/saves.test.ts` "a write that is on its way when the board is lost changes nothing when it answers" | all |
| D-SV-16 | A save that is in flight when the page unloads goes on (the call keeps its `keepalive`). | `test/api.test.ts` "a board's scene read gives its revision from the answer, 0 for none; a save keeps the call's own keepalive and signal", `desktop/test/ipcface.test.js` "a call goes on when the page unloads, and the id is free again after the answer" | desktop only |
| D-SV-17 | The stored file is an Excalidraw v2 scene with the elements, the files and the background colour (not scroll or zoom), so agents and the export read the same colours whatever the theme. | none | all |

### D.3 What an agent's change does on the open board (AG)

| ID | Behaviour (user-visible) | Covered today by | Note for the app |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-AG-01 | An element an agent adds with `apply` appears on the open board with no reload, and is saved. | `web/e2e/app.e2e.mjs` step 5 (paid) "Claude board chat draws client → server → database cleanly", step 7 (paid) "…the edit lands on arch", `test/saves.test.ts` "the tools of a section's agent name a board by its own id on that server, never by the page's key" (an `apply` update on a stored scene, not a drawing on a real canvas) | web view, device test (the web view applies a change) |
| D-AG-02 | What an agent creates starts clean unless it asks otherwise: roughness 0, sharp corners, the normal font; "hand", "normal" and "code" fonts, "round" corners and "artist" or "cartoonist" roughness are taken when asked. | `web/e2e/app.e2e.mjs` step 5 (paid) "the rectangles are sharp and not rough", "the text is in the normal font" | web view |
| D-AG-03 | Labels on shapes and arrows are bound text, centred and measured; an arrow between two existing shapes runs edge to edge, bound to both, and follows them when they move. | `web/e2e/app.e2e.mjs` step 5 (paid) "the boxes read client, server and database" | web view |
| D-AG-04 | A create with a key that exists updates that element instead (an upsert); a key that names an element of another type is refused (BAD_PARAMS). | none | web view |
| D-AG-05 | An update moves (`moveBy`, x, y), resizes, changes text or style; bound text follows its container, a style change reaches the bound label, a re-labelled container grows, arrows are re-routed. | none | web view |
| D-AG-06 | A change that names a version (`ifVersion`) that is not the current one is not applied and is reported as a conflict in the answer. | none | web view |
| D-AG-07 | A call with a bad reference or bad parameters (BAD_REF, BAD_PARAMS) changes nothing: everything is checked before anything is applied. | none | web view |
| D-AG-08 | An id an agent shortened to a unique prefix is taken for the element; an ambiguous or unknown one is an error. | `test/saves.test.ts` "get_image refs: an id shortened to a unique prefix works as in apply; an ambiguous or unknown one is UNKNOWN_REF" (the `get_image` side only) | web view |
| D-AG-09 | `select` and `focus` of an `apply` select the named elements and scroll them into view. | none | web view |
| D-AG-10 | ⌘Z removes the agent's whole change in one step (a call is one undo step, not one per element). | none | web view, wide only for the key; touch: the undo button |
| D-AG-11 | `delete_elements` deletes the elements and their bound text; with `cascade` the arrows bound to them go too and their labels with them, without it those arrows are unbound; the children of a deleted frame are freed. | none | web view |
| D-AG-12 | Where an agent worked is outlined on the open board for a moment, tagged with the agent's name ("Claude", "Cursor", "pi"), and with the chat's branch name ("Claude · <branch>") once the chat has more than one branch. | `web/e2e/app.e2e.mjs` step 5 (paid) "the agent outline shows where it drew", `test/attribution.test.ts` "editLabel: the agent's short name without a branch name", "editLabel: the agent and the branch name", "branchNameFor: a branch of a chat that split is named as the header names it", "branchNameFor: a chat with one branch gives no branch part", "branchNameFor: an unknown branch id gives no branch part", "branchNameFor: no tree, no branch, or a listed branch with nothing of its own yet gives no branch part" | web view |
| D-AG-13 | The outline sits on the changed elements in board coordinates, so it follows pan and zoom, and shows only for the board on screen; what an agent deleted is marked "Removed" in the danger tone. | none | web view, touch: the outline follows a pinch |

| D-AG-15 | An edit by an agent is saved soon after (within about a second), so the file on the server has it. | `web/e2e/app.e2e.mjs` step 5 (paid) "drawing.excalidraw on disk has the elements within about a second" | all |
| D-AG-16 | An agent's change to an archived board is refused (ARCHIVED). | none | all |

### D.10 Calls, the stream and cancelling, over IPC and over HTTP (TR)

| ID | Behaviour (user-visible) | Covered today by | Note for the app |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-TR-01 | A call over IPC answers the same as over HTTP: status, body, scene revision, refusals, empty and plain bodies, bytes; and asks the client the same. | `test/transport.test.ts` "the same requests give the same answers over IPC and over HTTP: status, body, scene revision, refusals, empty and plain bodies, bytes", `desktop/test/faces.test.js` "a call of every kind gives the same status, text and scene revision through both faces, and asks the client the same" | desktop (IPC); phone and tablet: the native transport answers the same (goal 4.6) |

| D-TR-03 | Cancelling a call: the app is told by the call's id; the page's promise rejects with the abort; a call aborted before it started makes no call; the answer of an aborted call is dropped; the app's call ends with 499 `cancelled`; the server is told to stop. | `test/transport.test.ts` "ipc call: aborting cancels the call in the app by its id and rejects with the abort; one aborted already makes no call; the answer of an aborted call is dropped", `desktop/test/ipcface.test.js` "cancel aborts the call with that id and answers it 499; other ids and other senders do not", `desktop/test/faces.test.js` "a call that is cancelled rejects with an AbortError through both faces and stops the client's call", `desktop/test/conn/calls.test.js` "a cancelled call rejects with AbortError and the server is told to stop", `web/dev/hostface.test.mjs` "the client's call is aborted when the request goes away before the answer" | all (goal 4.6: cancel works on every target; on the desktop it is carried as an identifier) |
| D-TR-04 | The way itself failing (the app did not answer) is a transport error and not a server's refusal; a server's refusal keeps its status, sentence and code. | `test/transport.test.ts` "ipc call: the way failing is a TransportError, not an answer", `test/api.test.ts` "a way that failed is no refusal: the transport's error comes as it is", "a refusal keeps the server's sentence and code; one without a sentence has the status in its words", "a refusal of a section's call keeps the status, the sentence and the code the app answered", `desktop/test/ipcface.test.js` "call: an answer of the client that is a refusal passes as it is" | all |
| D-TR-05 | A call names a server of the list and a path of the client API and nothing else: an unknown server is 404 `no_server`, a path outside the API 400 `not_client_api`, a body over its limit refused; nothing is sent. | `desktop/e2e/app.e2e.mjs` step "a call names a server of the list and a path of the client API, and nothing else", `desktop/test/conn/calls.test.js` "a server that is not in the list, or is the local one with no local entry: 404 no_server, nothing sent", "a path outside the client API is refused before anything is sent", "body limits: JSON 2 MiB, a scene 128 MiB, an image 5 MiB; nothing is sent over", `web/dev/host.test.mjs` "a call or a picture for an id that is no entry is 404 no_server, and nothing is sent anywhere", "a path outside the client API is 400 not_client_api, and is not sent", `desktop/test/ipcface.test.js` "call: a request of the wrong shape is rejected and not passed on" | all |
| D-TR-06 | A server that is not connected answers a call 503 `server_unreachable` with nothing sent; no answer in time is 504 `no_answer` (for a message, saying it is not known whether it arrived); an answer that is not JSON is 502; each route has its time limit; a server that dies in the middle of a call is 504 and the connection goes on; a refused secret (401) ends as 503 and the entry shows "secret not accepted". | `desktop/test/conn/calls.test.js` "an entry that is not connected: 503 server_unreachable and nothing sent; unfollow and release answer without it", "no answer in time: 504 no_answer; a message says it is not known whether it arrived", "an answer that is not JSON, or not UTF-8: 502 bad_answer", "the time limit of each route of the table", "a server that dies in the middle of a call: 504 no_answer, and the entry goes on from there", "a call answered 401: 503 server_unreachable, and the entry shows secret_not_accepted" | all |
| D-TR-07 | A call goes with the server's secret, the app's id and its feature level; the page never holds the secret, and no secret is in anything the page saw. | `desktop/e2e/app.e2e.mjs` step "a call goes to the server with the secret, and the answer comes back", "the page was never given the secret", "every secret stays out of everything the page saw", `test/api.test.ts` "a call is a server, a method, a path and a body: no client id or header of the page's goes with it" | all (the phone keeps the secret in secure storage, D11) |
| D-TR-08 | Many calls at once share one session and each is answered with its own answer. | `desktop/test/conn/calls.test.js` "many calls at once share one session and are answered each its own" | all |
| D-TR-09 | The stream opens with the greeting in order: "open", the list of servers, then per server its `hello` and `snapshot`, then connected; the secret is in none of it. | `desktop/e2e/app.e2e.mjs` step "this computer connects: the greeting, then connected, with no secret in it", `desktop/test/conn/connection.test.js` "connected: hello and the snapshot as the server sent them, then connected, then the events in order", "events before the snapshot (the hello) come after it is read whole, in order", `test/transport.test.ts` "ipc stream: the window is asked to subscribe after the page listens; envelopes come in the order of the batches; close lets go of both" | all |
| D-TR-10 | A stream that is cut comes back: the server shows as not connected at once, nothing of it arrives until its next `hello`, then the greeting comes again (hello, snapshot), it is connected again and its events come in order; the page is not loaded again. | `desktop/e2e/app.e2e.mjs` step "a stream that is cut comes back: not connected, then the greeting again, then connected", `desktop/test/conn/connection.test.js` "a cut: unreachable at once, no event of the entry until the next hello, then connected again", "a stream the server ends politely: unreachable \"The connection to the server ended\", then again", `desktop/test/faces.test.js` "the stream gives the same envelopes in the same order and the same notices through both faces, across a cut and a return", `web/e2e/localserver.e2e.mjs` step 3 | all (the shared connection code, goal 4.6; the phone's stream is read as text chunks) |
| D-TR-11 | A stream silent for the silence limit (35 s) counts as ended; pings keep it alive; retries come at growing intervals (1, 2, 5, 10, 30 s) and start again from the first step after a stable stream; a refused secret and a changed certificate end the trying until the entry changes. | `desktop/test/conn/connection.test.js` "silence: no byte for the silence limit ends the stream; pings keep it", "back-off: a refused entry is asked again by itself, ever less often, and Retry (a call) asks at once", "a stream that stayed open for the stable time starts the back-off again at its first step", "stopped states wait for the user: a refused secret is not asked again until an edit or a test", "a changed certificate stops until it is accepted; accept connects with the new pin" | all (the same cases against fake ports on the phone, goal 7.2) |
| D-TR-12 | A stream closed for good (the app quits, the window refused the subscription) is one notice "closed": every server shows as not connected and the page opens the stream again a moment later, once. | `test/transport.test.ts` "ipc stream: a subscription the window refused is a stream closed for good", `test/conn.test.ts` "a stream the transport closed for good: every entry shows as not connected, and the stream is opened again a moment later", "a stream closed for good twice before it is opened again is opened once; connect() meanwhile takes the wait away", `desktop/test/faces.test.js` "a stream that is closed for good gives the page one notice \"closed\" through both faces, and opening it again greets it again" | all |
| D-TR-13 | Opening the stream again ends the first one and what the older one still says is dropped; the new one has its own listeners. | `test/conn.test.ts` "the stream is opened through the transport, and opening it again closes the one before", "what an older stream says after a newer one was opened is dropped", `test/transport.test.ts` "a stream is opened again after a close: the new one has its own listeners and the old one none", `desktop/test/ipcface.test.js` "stream: opening again ends the first subscription, and what it still says is dropped" | all |
| D-TR-14 | Events reach the page in order, in batches of at most 500 per turn; nothing is sent to a window that is gone. | `desktop/test/ipcface.test.js` "stream: opens a subscription, tells the page \"open\", and sends the envelopes in order, batched per turn", "stream: a batch holds at most 500 envelopes, and all of them arrive in order", "stream: nothing is sent to a window that is gone" | desktop only |
| D-TR-15 | The window's subscription ends when its page navigates away, reloads, crashes or the window closes; a navigation inside the page or of a frame inside it keeps it. | `desktop/test/ipcface.test.js` "stream: the subscription ends when the main frame navigates, reloads, crashes, or the window closes", "stream: a navigation inside the page, or of a frame inside it, keeps the subscription" | desktop only |
| D-TR-16 | The app's own operations (the server list, the owner's things) answer the same status and body over IPC as over HTTP; an operation outside the list is 404 `no_op` and reaches nothing. | `desktop/test/faces.test.js` "the app operations answer the same status and body through both faces; the two the host has no shell for differ, and say so", `desktop/test/ipcface.test.js` "app: a listed operation goes to the client with its arguments; the body comes back as JSON text", "app: operations outside the list answer 404 no_op and reach nothing" | desktop only (the owner's things exist for this computer only) |
| D-TR-17 | Only the app's own window and page is answered on every channel: another window, a frame inside the page, a page that has gone elsewhere gets nothing. | `desktop/test/ipcface.test.js` "a caller that is not the app's own page (…) gets nothing from any channel", "a hash on the page's URL does not turn the page away", "every channel is registered, and removed again by dispose" | desktop only |
| D-TR-18 | The window gives the page the seven functions (`call`, `cancel`, `stream`, `onEvents`, `onStreamState`, `app`, `pickImages`) and nothing else; listeners get the payload and never the IPC event, and can be removed. | `desktop/test/preload.test.js` "preload: the page gets window.aiwbApp with seven functions and nothing else", "preload: cancel, stream and app go over their channels with a fixed shape", "preload: onEvents and onStreamState hand the page the payload, never the IPC event, and unsubscribe" | desktop only |
| D-TR-19 | The page has one way to the network, chosen at start: IPC when the window gave its functions, HTTP when loaded over http (the test host), none otherwise ("AI Whiteboard runs in its app."); nothing outside the transport folder calls fetch, an event source or the app's functions. | `test/transport.test.ts` "the way is IPC when the window gave the page its functions, HTTP when the page was loaded over http, none otherwise", "transport(): the one the page has, the same each time; none is an error that names the app; a test puts its own in the place", "nothing of the page outside src/transport reaches the network, the app's functions or the host: no fetch, no EventSource, no aiwbApp, no /host/, no /api/on, no aiwbDesktop" | all (one transport per platform, goal 4.6) |
| D-TR-20 | A call id in use, a bad call body and a bad stream request are rejected; the id is free again after the answer. | `desktop/test/ipcface.test.js` "call: an id that is in use is rejected", "call: a request of the wrong shape is rejected and not passed on", "a call goes on when the page unloads, and the id is free again after the answer" | desktop only |
| D-TR-21 | The test host's HTTP face does what the main process does: a call, one stream and pictures over plain HTTP on the loopback, answers its own page alone and holds no secret. | `web/dev/hostface.test.mjs` (a call, the stream, a picture, "it answers its own page alone: a Host that is not a loopback name, or an Origin that is not its own, is 403"), `web/dev/host.test.mjs` "it listens on 127.0.0.1 alone: its address, and no other address of the machine reaches it", "calls, tests, a picture and every list operation, and no body or header holds the secret" | web build in tests only (never shipped) |

### D.11 The desktop window (DW)

| ID | Behaviour (user-visible) | Covered today by | Note for the app |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-DW-01 | The window opens at once from the page file whatever the server does (the page waits for no server), titled "AI Whiteboard"; the page sees `window.aiwbApp` and no Node (`require`, `process`, `Buffer`); a page error does not occur. | `desktop/e2e/app.e2e.mjs` step "the window opens at once, from the page file, whatever the server does", step "the page raised no uncaught error" | desktop only (the window loads the web build, goal D2) |
| D-DW-02 | Closing the window while a drawing waits to be saved waits for the write, then closes with no dialog. | `desktop/test/closeflush.test.js` "close with pending saves: flush, then close again without a dialog" | desktop only |
| D-DW-03 | ⌘Q while a drawing waits: the quit waits for the write, then quits. | `desktop/test/closeflush.test.js` "Cmd+Q with pending saves: flush, then app.quit()", "before-quit is registered once per app, however many windows" | desktop only |
| D-DW-04 | Reloading the window while a drawing waits: the reload waits for the write, then reloads. | `desktop/test/closeflush.test.js` "reload with pending saves: flush, then reload" | desktop only |
| D-DW-05 | When the write does not finish within 10 s (server down), the dialog "Board changes are not saved. The server did not answer. Close anyway and lose them?" offers Cancel and Close anyway; Cancel leaves the window open and does nothing more; Close anyway closes (or quits) and lets the next unload through. | `desktop/test/closeflush.test.js` "FLUSH_TIMEOUT_MS is 10 s", "flush that never finishes: dialog, Cancel does nothing more", "flush that never finishes: Close anyway closes and lets the next unload through", "flush that never finishes on Cmd+Q: Close anyway quits", "Cmd+Q, flush that never finishes, Cancel: no quit, no close, no reload" | desktop only |
| D-DW-06 | A flush that answers false, rejects or throws is treated as not flushed (the same dialog); a cancelled close or quit is forgotten, so the next unload alone is a reload. | `desktop/test/closeflush.test.js` "executeJavaScript rejects: treated as not flushed", "executeJavaScript throws: treated as not flushed", "flush resolving false (saves still pending) is treated as not flushed", "a cancelled close or quit is forgotten: the next unload alone is a reload" | desktop only |
| D-DW-07 | With no drawing waiting, closing, quitting and reloading are immediate and the dialog never shows. | none | desktop only |
| D-DW-08 | Attach in the composer opens the app's own open dialog "Add images" (button "Add") on the main window, for several files of the types a chat takes (PNG, JPEG, GIF, WebP). | `desktop/test/pickimages.test.js` "the dialog is the app's open dialog on the main window, for several image files", "the limits and types are the server's", "imageType: by extension, whatever its case; null for anything else", `test/transport.test.ts` "ipc: the address of a picture is the app's scheme with the server, the chat and the image, each one segment; the app's operations go through; the dialog is the window's" | desktop only |
| D-DW-09 | The dialog's files come back with their base name (never a path), their type by extension and their bytes, in the order the dialog gave them, and go through the same check as a pasted file. | `desktop/test/pickimages.test.js` "what the dialog returns: each file's base name, type and bytes, in the dialog's order", `test/chatimage.test.ts` "what the desktop app's dialog returns goes through the check as any file, and the files it left out are named" | desktop only |
| D-DW-10 | A cancelled dialog adds nothing and says nothing. | `desktop/test/pickimages.test.js` "a cancelled dialog: both lists empty, nothing opened", `test/chatimage.test.ts` "a cancelled dialog, and an answer of another shape, give nothing" | desktop only |
| D-DW-11 | A file the dialog returned but the app left out is named in the composer's error line with its reason: another type (never opened), more than ten (the first ten are read), cannot be read or is no file, or over 5 MB (its bytes are not read; one that grew past the limit while read is left out too); a file left out takes no place from the ones after it. | `desktop/test/pickimages.test.js` "a file over the size limit is left out as \"size\" and none of it is read", "a file that grew past the limit after its size was seen: \"size\", at most one byte over is read", "more files than the count limit: the first ten are read, the rest left out as \"count\" unopened", "a file left out for its size, type or a failed read takes no place of the count", "a file of another type is left out as \"type\" and never opened", "a file that cannot be read, or is no file, is left out as \"read\"", `test/chatimage.test.ts` "the error line says why each file was not added, naming it, one sentence per reason" | desktop only |
| D-DW-12 | The dialog opens only for the app's own window and page (not for another window, a frame or a page that has gone elsewhere, also not when the page went away while the dialog was open); the page names no path. | `desktop/test/pickimages.test.js` "a caller that is not the app's own window (…): refused, no dialog, nothing read", "the caller stops being the app's own window while the dialog is open (…): refused, nothing read", "no main window, or no page URL: refused, no dialog", "ipcface: the dialog is answered through pickImages, with the main window, the page URL and no argument of the page", `desktop/test/preload.test.js` "preload: pickImages takes no argument and names no path" | desktop only |
| D-DW-13 | On a phone or a tablet the attach button opens the system's image picker, and what it returns goes through the same check. | none | touch: the system's image picker; device test (goal 7.4) |
| D-DW-14 | A link in a chat or on a whiteboard element (`window.open`) opens in the user's default browser and never in an app window; only http, https and mailto are opened; other schemes (file, javascript, data, the picture scheme) and unparsable addresses are dropped. | `desktop/test/links.test.js` "linkAction: other origins with http, https or mailto → open", "linkAction: other schemes and unparsable URLs → drop", "window.open of an allowed URL → openExternal once, deny", "window.open of a file: URL → no openExternal, deny" | desktop only; phone and tablet: the system's browser |
| D-DW-15 | The window never leaves the page: a navigation to another page is prevented and, when allowed, opened in the default browser; the page itself, with or without a hash, may be navigated to; another file, the page with a query, or a picture address is prevented and not opened. | `desktop/test/links.test.js` "linkAction: the page itself, with or without a hash → stay", "linkAction: another file, the page with a query, or a picture address → drop", "will-navigate to another origin → prevented and opened outside", "will-navigate to a disallowed scheme → prevented, not opened", "will-navigate to the page with another hash → neither prevented nor opened", "will-navigate to a reload of another file or to the picture scheme → prevented, not opened", "setPageURL moves the page the window stays on" | desktop only |
